Optimization loop with CG and mult:
Pseudo top-level:
```
def optimize_cg_mult(p0, rng, budget, verbose=False):
    p = sanitize(p0)
    best=p; best_score=score(p)
    cur=best_score
    deadline=...
    beta=100; power=5; top_frac=1/n?; mu=1.25
    it=0; stall=0
    while time < deadline:
       c = conv(p,p)
       max_c = c.max(); score = 2n max_c (since sum1)
       # weights
       if rng.random<0.5: use top m with random m
       else: softmax/power
       # compute W_fft, diag, Qa
       # first-order mult candidate with h
       # CG candidate direction
       # line search over candidate transforms
       # accept if improves cur (or best)
       # adapt mu, beta, power, top
    return best
```

def _make_q_matrix_data(w, L):
    n = len? w length 2n-1; get n? We can pass n.
    diag = w[0:2*n-1:2] # w[2i]
    return np.fft.rfft(w, L), diag, L
```
`_matvec_q(x, W, diag, L)`:
```
xr = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
R = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(xr), L)
return 0.5*(R[:len(x)] + diag * x)
```
For CG, need repeated rfft(x). Each matvec also rfft(x). Could we cache rfft of p? p changes. no.

def _solve_q_system(w, a, mu, max_iter=12, tol=1e-7):
    n=len(a); L=_next_pow2(3*n+2); W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); diag=w[0:2*n-1:2]
    def? no nested. Use for loop.
    Qa = _matvec_q(a, W, diag, L)
    b = -Qa
    # precondition diag_A = mu + (w[2i]/2? Wait matvec_A(x)=Qx+μx. Diagonal Q_ii = w[2i]. So diagA=μ + w[2i]
    diagA = mu + diag
    x = zeros; r=b; z=r/diagA; rho=np.dot(z,r); p=z
    d=zeros
    for it in range(max_iter):
       Ap=_matvec_q(p,W,diag,L)+mu*p
       pAp=np.dot(p,Ap)
       if pAp <= 1e-30: break
       alpha=rho/pAp
       d += alpha*p
       r_new = r - alpha*Ap
       # check
       rho_new=np.dot(z,r_new)
       r=r_new
       normr=np.sqrt(np.dot(r,r))
       if normr <= tol*np.sqrt(np.dot(b,b)): break
       if rho_new <=0: p=z # or break
       else: p=z + (rho_new/rho)*p; rho=rho_new
    return d, Qa, normr
```
But in preconditioned CG, after x update, r = r - alpha Ap. z fixed. rho = r·z. beta = rho_new/rho. Good.
Need initial p if b norm zero. If Qa zero (w all zero) no.
Potential problem: A may not be SPD if μ not enough; pAp could be negative. If negative, break and use gradient direction. We can set μ=1.5 to ensure pAp? Since Q eigen within [-1,1]; μ=1.5 => A min 0.5. Numerical with row sum bound should be. But w length 2n-1, Q row sum maybe? Let's bound row sum = Qii + 0.5 ∑_{j≠i} w_{i+j}. Since sum w=1, row sum ≤1? For i=0 with w0 maybe? Row sum = w0 +0.5∑_{1}^{n-1} w_j ≤1 (if w0 plus half others). For i such that all w in window, row sum =? <=1+? If w_{2i}=1 and w? no because sum=1. Max 1. Spectral norm of symmetric nonnegative with row sum≤1 ≤1. Good. μ=1.5 safe. Use μ options >1.0.

Line search functions:
```
def _line_search_additive(a, d, score):
    cur = score(a)
    best_p=a; best_sc=cur
    dsum = d.sum()
    # center d maybe d - dsum*a? Because sum a=1
    # But if we change d, line search: z=a+alpha d_eff. Choose d_eff = d - dsum*a to zero sum.
    d0 = d - dsum*a
    # Cap max alpha to avoid too negative:
    neg = d0 < -1e-15
    if any: alpha_max = 0.95 * min(a[pos]/(-d0[pos])) else inf
    max_alpha = min(1.0, alpha_max)
    alphas = [max_alpha, max_alpha*0.5, ...]
    for alpha in alphas:
       z = a + alpha*d0
       z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
       if not finite: continue
       s=z.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue
       z=z/s
       sc=score(z)
       if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12): update
    return best_p,best_sc
```
But if d all positive/negative, d0 will have both unless d proportional a. Good.
Maybe try additive without centering too? Normalization handles but d0 ensures sum zero and maybe better. We can try both: candidates z = a+αd and z=a+α(d-dsum*a). But dsum*a centered.
To reduce evaluations, evaluate up to 5 candidates.
Maybe use golden ratio? Fixed halving enough.

Multiplicative gradient fallback:
```
def _mult_step(a, h, alphas):
   hpos = np.maximum(h, 0?) Actually gradient h nonnegative? w and a nonnegative, R_i=∑w a >=0, so Qa>=0. h positive. To minimize, decrease coordinates with high h. Factor exp(-α h_norm).
   hmax = h.max(); if tiny return.
   rel = -alphas*h/hmax
   z=a*exp(rel); floor.
```
Since h positive, factor <1. Norm? If we center? Perhaps `rel = -(h/hmax - mean(h/hmax))` to allow increasing some? But objective L∞, gradient says all coordinates contribute with nonnegative weights; decreasing high gradient. Since sum normalization will increase all, effectively increase low h. Good.

Weights generation:
- c full length. `cmax = c.max()`. If cmax <=0, uniform.
- Method random:
  * `hard_top`: choose m = int(max(1, n * rint? [0.001,0.01,0.05]?)) maybe 8,32,128,256. Use idx top m, weights = c[idx] / sum.
  * `power`: r = c / cmax; p = choose [2,5,10,30,100,1000]. w=r**p/sum. To avoid underflow: if p large and r<~1, exp(p log r). Use `np.power`. For p=1000 zeros; okay.
  * `softmax`: beta choose [20,50,100,200,500,1000,3000]. x = n*c / cmax? Better normalized r. softmax on r*beta: exp(beta*(r-1)). Since rmax=1. Use shift. w=e/sum. This is smooth top.
  * `threshold`: set weights = c where c >= (1-ε)*max, else ε? Choose thr=0.85,0.95.
Maybe combine by randomly selecting from these.

At optimum, many peaks near max, so all methods similar. During initial, hard max may reduce local peaks. Use mostly top/power with high p.

Adaptive schedule in optimizer:
```
def _optimize_sequence(a0, rng, budget, verbose=False, min_improve=1e-12):
   p=sanitize(a0); cur=score(p); best=p; best_sc=cur
   deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
   it=0; stall=0; no_best=0
   mode_power=10; beta=100; mu=1.35; cg_iter=10
   while time<deadline and stall<1000?:
       c=_full_conv(p,p)
       # if cur stale? cur=score
       # weights
       method=rng.random()
       if method<0.45: power weights with power = choose increasing with stall
       elif <0.8: top m
       else softmax
       # compute Qa, h=2Qa for mult
       # CG solve; get d
       # build candidate list
       # evaluate up to maybe 6 candidates: CG alphas 0.75,0.5,0.25; mult alphas 0.02,0.05,0.1
       # pick best
       if improved current:
           p=...; cur=...
           if cur<best_sc: best=...
           stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
           # perturb
           if stall>3 and rng.random<0.2: p=best.copy(); cur=best_sc
           if stall>10 and rng.random<0.1: p= p * exp(rng.normal(0, sigma)); sanitize; accept if not too bad
           # adapt mu: if line search failed due negative, increase mu
       it++
   return best,best_sc
```

Implement `_full_conv(a, L=None)`:
```
def _full_conv(a, L=None):
    n=len(a); nout=2*n-1
    if L is None: L=_next_pow2(nout)
    # If n small use np.convolve? maybe for exact and speed
    if n <= 256: return np.convolve(a,a)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
    return c[:nout]
```
Need ensure numerical negative small due FFT; score uses max, small negative not issue. For full conv using A*A (not conj) (convolution, not correlation). Good. For matvec_J using `W*np.conj(X)`? def _sanitize_nonneg(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z=np.maximum(z,0.0)
    return z

Now, parametric initialization maybe enough. Let's write functions:
```
def _init_uniform(n): return np.ones(n)/n

def _init_poly_sym(n, coeffs):
    x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n*2 - 1
    p = np.ones(n)
    x2 = x*x
    for coeff, deg in coeffs:
       p += coeff * (x2 ** (deg//2))
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-6); return p/p.sum()

def _init_trig_sym(n, coeffs):
    t = np.arange(n)/float(n)
    p = np.ones(n)
    for amp, mode in coeffs: p += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*mode*t)
    # maybe mode=1 can make center low
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-6); return p/p.sum()
```

Need have robust `_resample(p, n_new)`: linear interpolation with 0.5 centers. Add optional small noise? Could use deterministic.
```
def _resample(p, n_new):
    p= _sanitize(p); n=len(p)
    if n==n_new: return p.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n; x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    q=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p); q=np.maximum(q,0); q=q/q.sum(); return q
```
For larger n, this may create oscillations? no.

Pseudo code for `_optimize_sequence`:
```
def _optimize_sequence(p0, rng, budget_s, verbose=False):
    p = _sanitize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=len(p)
    Lfull=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Lcorr=_next_pow2(3*n+4)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=_score_seq(p)
    cur=best_sc
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    it=0; stall=0
    method_idx=0
    power=8.0; beta=100.0; top_m=max(8, n//128); mu=1.25
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=_full_conv(p, Lfull)
        # If cmax infinite? break
        # choose method
        if it % 20 == 0:
            # random adapt
            method = rng.integers(0,4)
            if method==0: power=... top_m=...
        # But every iteration? Could vary slowly.
        if it % 10 == 0: method...
        w = _make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m)
        row = _row_sums(w,n)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, Lcorr)
        R = _matvec_J(p, W, Lcorr)
        C = float(np.dot(p,R))
        # objective score exact
        cmax=float(c.max()); cur_sc=2*n*cmax
        if cur_sc < cur: cur=cur_sc
        # candidates list? Evaluate minimal.
        local_best=p; local_best_sc=cur_sc
        # Additive solve if reasonable
        delta = _cg_solve_J(W,row,p,R,C,mu,max_iter=12,tol=1e-7,L=Lcorr)
        # evaluate additive candidates
        dsum=float(delta.sum()) if finite else 0
        dctr = delta - p*dsum
        for dvar,alpha in [(delta,0.5),(delta,0.25),(dctr,1.0),(delta,1.0)]:
             if not finite delta: break
             z=p+alpha*dvar; sanitize; sc=score; update local
        # mult candidates
        rel0 = C - R  # positive low R
        scale0=float(np.max(np.abs(rel0)))
        if scale0>0:
            for eta in (0.1,0.3,1.0):
                rel=np.clip(eta*rel0/scale0, -3,3)
                z=p*np.exp(rel); z=np.maximum(z, 1e-18/n)
                z/=z.sum()
                sc=_score_seq(z,Lfull); update
        rel1 = p*(C-R)
        scale1=max(abs)
        if scale1>0:
            for eta in (0.3,1.0): similarly
        # Also previous h R-C? Equivalent.
        # choose best
        if local_best_sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
            p=local_best; cur=local_best_sc
            if cur < best_sc: best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur
            stall=0
        else:
            stall +=1
            # adapt
            if stall>15 and rng.random()<0.4:
                p=best.copy(); cur=best_sc
            if stall>30 and rng.random()<0.2:
                sigma=0.05+0.005*min(stall,50)
                z=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n)); z=np.maximum(z,0); z/=z.sum(); sc=score; if sc < best_sc*1.05: p=z; cur=sc
            # adjust mu
            if stall % 20 == 0:
                mu = min(2.5, mu*1.1) if no improvement? or random
        # adapt weights
        if stall>10 and rng.random()<0.5:
            method = int(rng.integers(0,4)); power=...; top_m=...
        it+=1
        if verbose and it%200==0: print
    return best,best_sc
```

Let's implement `_make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m)`:
```
def _make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m):
    m=len(c)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(m)/m
    if method == 0: # top
       mtop=int(top_m); mtop=max(1,min(m,mtop))
       if mtop == m: w=c.copy()
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -mtop)[-mtop:]
          w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=c[idx]
       s=w.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else w/=s; return w
    elif method ==1: # power
       r=c/cmax; # r[0..]
       r=np.where(r>0,r,0.0)
       if power <=1: w=r
       else: w=np.power(r, power)
       # add tiny uniform to avoid all zero
       w += 1e-12
       s=w.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else w/=s; return w
    elif method ==2:
       r=c/cmax
       e=np.exp(beta*(r-1.0))
       e += 1e-12
       s=e.sum(); ...
    else:
       eps=float(rng.choice([0.005,0.02,0.05,0.10,0.20]))
       thr=cmax*(1-eps)
       w=np.where(c>=thr,c,0.0)
       s=w.sum(); if s<=0: top1
```
Need no `rng.choice` with list? okay. But if rng numpy Generator has `choice`. Or use `float(rng.uniform(0.005,0.2))`.
For `np.argpartition` when mtop=m, error? It can but handle.
Add `w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)`.

Potential underflow in power=300: r<1, r**300 underflows to 0 for most, possibly all except max; okay. But if all underflow due cmax? cmax normalized 1; max power 300=max=1. Sum fine.

Now, `_cg_solve_J` details: We need to pass `row` precomputed. In PCG, pref = mu + row. But if row all zero? w normalized sum1, some row positive. Add eps.
```
def _cg_solve_J(W, row, p, R, C, mu, max_iter, tol, L):
    b = C - R
    b_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(b))
    if b_norm <= 1e-15: return None
    n=len(b)
    x=np.zeros_like(b)
    r=b.copy()
    pref=mu + row + 1e-12
    z=r/pref
    rho=float(np.dot(z,r))
    if rho <= 0 or not finite: return -b/mu # fallback
    d=np.zeros_like(b)
    pvec=z.copy()
    for it in range(max_iter):
       Ap=_matvec_J(pvec,W,L) + mu*pvec
       pdAp=float(np.dot(pvec, Ap))
       if pdAp <= 1e-30 or not finite: break
       alpha=rho/pdAp
       # if alpha too big? clip
       d += alpha*pvec
       r = r - alpha*Ap
       r_norm=float(np.linalg.norm(r))
       if r_norm <= tol*b_norm: break
       rho_new=float(np.dot(z,r))
       if rho_new <= 0 or not finite:
           pvec=z.copy()
       else:
           pvec = z + (rho_new/rho)*pvec
       rho=rho_new
    return d
```
Fallback if no CG: `-b/mu` maybe. Actually step direction to reduce R-C: δ ≈ -? We want Jδ=C-R=b. If μ large, δ≈b/μ. So fallback positive b/mu. `return b/mu` (not -). We'll return `b/mu` if CG fails. If b_norm finite. In optimizer, line search can handle negative or positive step; b/mu is direction to increase low R. Good.

Check sign: b=C-R; if R<C, b>0, need increase R by adding mass to low R coordinates? Jδ at that coordinate positive. So δ positive. Good.

But line search with z=p+αδ: if δ positive at low R, increases those coordinates; normalization redistributes. Good.

Multiplicative rel: `rel = C - R` (positive low R, negative high R). `z=p*exp(η*rel/scale)` increases low, decreases high. Same sign.

Additive candidate may make negative if δ negative large. We clip.

Potential problem: δ magnitude from CG with μ=1.2 can be larger than p (e.g., b~1/n, δ~1/n). α=1 may set many coordinates negative or huge. Line search with clipping may zero them; maybe α=0.25 safer. Try multiple. We can also cap δ to e.g. 10*p+1e-6? Not necessary but can reduce bad. Use `delta = np.clip(delta, -5.0/n*10, 5.0/n*10)`? Since p ~1/n; 10p=10/n=0.0003 for n=3072, δ maybe similar. Could clip to 50*median? Eh.

Maybe use multiplicative primarily and additive with small α. If additive fails, fallback mult.

Let's design `_random_init(n, rng, kind)`:
```
def _random_init(n, rng):
    kind = int(rng.integers(0,5))
    t = np.arange(n)/n
    if kind==0: A=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)); p=1 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*t); if rng.random<0.5 add B cos4p etc.
    elif kind==1: x=2*t-1; A=float(rng.uniform(0.1,5.0)); gamma=float(rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)); p=(1 + A*x*x)**gamma
    elif kind==2: mixture gaussians: base=0.05; K=1-3; centers in [0.01,0.25] and symmetric; widths 0.01-0.15; weights lognormal
    elif kind==3: symmetric step: e = rng.uniform(0.02,0.2); ratio=rng.uniform(2,20); p=np.ones(n); p[:e*n]=ratio; p[-e*n:]=ratio; noise.
    elif kind==4: parent-like if available? no.
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-6); return p/p.sum()
```

Random init function detailed:
```
def _random_initial(n, rng):
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 7))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / n
    if kind == 0:
        A = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.3))
        p = 1.0 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*t)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            B = float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)); B*=...; p += B*np.cos(4*np.pi*t)
    elif kind == 1:
        x = 2.0*t - 1.0
        A = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.0)); gamma=float(rng.uniform(0.3,3.0))
        p = (1.0 + A*x*x)**gamma
    elif kind == 2:
        p = np.ones(n)*0.05
        K=int(rng.integers(1,6))
        for ...:
           c=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); s=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.2)); w=float(rng.lognormal(0,1))
           # periodic? no, edge wrap for symmetric? Use min distance to edges? For arbitrary, use periodic to allow edges.
           d=np.minimum(np.abs(t-c), 1-np.abs(t-c))
           p += w*np.exp(-0.5*(d/s)**2)
    elif kind == 3:
        # random block
        K=int(rng.integers(2,8)); p=np.empty(n); fill random heights lognormal
    elif kind == 4:
        # two-level random
        k=int(n*rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); ratio=rng.uniform(1.5,6); p=np.ones(n); p[rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)]=ratio; noise
    elif kind == 5:
        # smooth random Fourier with phases
        p=np.ones(n)
        for m in range(1,6): amp=float(rng.normal(0,1.0/m)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); p += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+ph)
    else:
        # geometric/exponential gradient
        p=np.array([(np.exp(rng.normal(0,1)) for? not allowed? Use rng.lognormal) ...])
```
Need no generator expressions? Fine but maybe no lambda. Use `rng.lognormal(0, 1, n)`.
Ensure p≥1e-6 and normalize.
For asymmetric, add trend: p *= np.linspace(0.5,1.5,n) or random slope. Could be useful.

Detailed helper implementations:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    L=1
    while L<x: L <<=1
    return L
```
Could use `1 << (x-1).bit_length()` but loop okay. For x large, loop fine.

```
def _sanitize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim != 1: p=p.ravel()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    return p/s
```
For final, we might want not normalize? We'll normalize.

```
def _full_conv(a, L=None):
    n=len(a)
    nout=2*n-1
    if nout <=0: return np.array([])
    if L is None: L=_next_pow2(nout)
    if n <= 512:
        return np.convolve(a,a).astype(float)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
    return c[:nout].astype(float)
```
For n=512, np.convolve exact O(262k) maybe okay. For many evals, FFT faster? Use threshold maybe 256. Let's use 256. For n=512, FFT L=1024, fine. Use threshold 128.

```
def _score_seq(p, L=None):
    p=_sanitize(p) # might allocate and normalize; for speed, in candidates already normalized. But okay.
    if p is None: return inf
    c=_full_conv(p,L)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    mx=float(c.max())
    if mx < 0: mx=0.0
    return float(2.0*len(p)*mx)
```
Since p sum1. If we call with p sum not 1 after sanitize, score includes sum? `_score_seq` as 2n max; assumes sum1. If not, sanitize normalizes. Good.

```
def _matvec_J(x, W, L):
    n=len(x)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    y=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(X), L)
    return y[:n]
```

```
def _row_sums_J(w, n):
    # w len 2n-1
    pref=np.empty(len(w)+1, dtype=float)
    pref[0]=0.0
    np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
    return pref[n:2*n] - pref[:n]
```
Works for len(w)+1, pref[1:]=len(w). Need ensure 2*n <= len(w)+1: len(w)=2n-1, len+1=2n. pref[n:2*n] length n. Good.

```
def _make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m):
    m=len(c)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(m)/m
    w=np.zeros(m)
    if method ==0:
       mtop=int(np.clip(top_m,1,m))
       if mtop < m:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, m-mtop)[-mtop:] # np.argpartition(c, -mtop) also
          w[idx]=c[idx]
       else:
          w=c.copy()
    elif method ==1:
       r=c/cmax
       r=np.where(r>0, r, 0.0)
       if power <=1.0:
          w=r.copy()
       else:
          w=np.power(r, float(power))
       w += 1e-12
    elif method ==2:
       r=c/cmax
       # beta can be large; r-1 <=0
       e=np.exp(float(beta)*(r-1.0))
       w=e + 1e-12
    else:
       eps=float(rng.uniform(0.001,0.25))
       thr=cmax*(1.0-eps)
       w=np.where(c >= thr, c, 0.0)
    s=float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       w=np.ones(m); s=m
    w/=s
    return w
```
Need `np.clip` returns scalar; okay. `np.argpartition(c, m-mtop)` because kth index 0-based; to get largest mtop, kth=m-mtop. If mtop=m, skip. If mtop=1, kth=m-1, works. Could use `-mtop`? NumPy argpartition accepts negative? Yes but not sure. Use positive.

```
def _cg_solve_J(W, row, R, C, mu, max_iter, tol, L):
    n=len(R)
    b = C - R
    b_norm=float(np.linalg.norm(b))
    if b_norm <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    x=np.zeros(n)
    r=b.copy()
    pref=mu + row + 1e-12
    pref=np.where(pref>1e-12, pref, 1.0)
    z=r/pref
    rho=float(np.dot(z,r))
    if not np.isfinite(rho) or rho <= 1e-300:
       return b/mu
    pvec=z.copy()
    delta=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
       Ap=_matvec_J(pvec, W, L) + mu*pvec
       pAp=float(np.dot(pvec, Ap))
       if not np.isfinite(pAp) or pAp <= 1e-300:
          break
       alpha=rho/pAp
       if not np.isfinite(alpha): break
       # clip alpha to avoid crazy? Maybe alpha_max = 10.0/mu? But b norm small.
       delta += alpha*pvec
       r_new = r - alpha*Ap
       r_norm=float(np.linalg.norm(r_new))
       r = r_new
       if not np.isfinite(r_norm): break
       if r_norm <= tol*b_norm:
          break
       rho_new=float(np.dot(z,r))
       if not np.isfinite(rho_new) or rho_new <= 1e-300:
          pvec=z.copy()
          rho=0.0
          break
       beta=rho_new/rho
       pvec = z + beta*pvec
       rho=rho_new
    return delta
```
If rho becomes 0, break returning partial delta. Good.

Candidate eval function top-level:
```
def _candidate_score(p, L):
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return p, inf
    p=p/s
    c=_full_conv(p,L)
    mx=c.max()
    sc=2*len(p)*mx if mx>0 else inf
    return p, float(sc) if finite else inf
```
But returning tuple; okay. In optimizer we can call. To avoid re-sanitize too much, implement `_eval_candidate(z,L)` returns normalized p and score.
```
def _eval_candidate(z, L):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size==0: return None, inf
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
    z=np.maximum(z,0.0)
    s=float(z.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return None, inf
    z=z/s
    c=_full_conv(z,L)
    if c.size==0: return None, inf
    mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx<0: mx=max(mx,0.0)
    sc=float(2.0*len(z)*mx)
    return z, sc
```
When p sum 1.

Optimizer function:
```
def _optimize_sequence(p0, rng, budget_s, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize(p0)
    if p is None: return p0, inf
    n=len(p)
    Lfull=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Lcorr=_next_pow2(3*n+4)
    cur=float(_score_seq(p, Lfull))
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur; best_time=time.monotonic()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    it=0; stall=0
    method=1; power=8.0; beta=50.0; top_m=max(4,int(n/256)); mu=1.15
    floor=1e-18/ max(1,n)
    if verbose: print(f"   opt start n={n} score={cur:.6f} budget={budget_s:.0f}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c=_full_conv(p, Lfull)
        cmax=float(c.max())
        if cmax <=0: cur=inf? break
        cur_sc=float(2*n*cmax)
        # If p not sum 1? It is.
        # Adaptive parameters every 7 iter
        if it % 7 == 0:
            # base method
            u=rng.random()
            if u < 0.35: method=1; power=float(rng.choice([3,5,8,12,20,40,80,160]))
            elif u < 0.6: method=0; top_m=int(np.clip(int(n * rng.choice([0.0005,0.001,0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05])),1,n))
            elif u < 0.85: method=2; beta=float(rng.choice([10,20,50,100,300,800,2000]))
            else: method=3
            # mu adaptive
            if stall > 15:
                mu=float(rng.choice([1.05,1.1,1.2,1.4]))
            else:
                mu=float(rng.choice([1.08,1.15,1.3,1.6]))
        # make weights
        w=_make_weights(c,n,rng,method,power,beta,top_m)
        # if w all zeros? fixed
        row=_row_sums_J(w,n)
        W=np.fft.rfft(w, Lcorr)
        R=_matvec_J(p, W, Lcorr)
        R=np.where(np.isfinite(R), R, 0.0)
        C=float(np.dot(w,c))
        if not np.isfinite(C) or C<=0: C=float(np.dot(p,R)) if finite else 0
        local_p=p; local_sc=cur_sc
        # Additive CG
        max_cg = 16 if n <=4096 else 10
        delta = _cg_solve_J(W,row,R,C,mu,max_cg,1e-8,Lcorr)
        if delta is not None and np.any(np.abs(delta)>0):
            d=np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, 0.0)
            dsum=float(d.sum())
            dctr=d - p*dsum
            # Trim huge values?
            med=float(np.median(p))
            maxd=50.0*max(med, 1e-12)
            d=np.clip(d, -maxd, maxd)
            dctr=np.clip(dctr, -maxd, maxd)
            # evaluate alphas
            for dv, alpha in ((dctr, 1.0), (d, 0.5), (dctr,0.5), (d,0.25), (d,1.0)):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0: break
                z = p + alpha*dv
                zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, Lfull)
                if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                    local_p=zz; local_sc=sc
        # Mulle candidates
        # rel0 = C - R (condition)
        rel0 = C - R
        scale0=float(np.max(np.abs(rel0)))
        if scale0 > 1e-300:
            rel=np.clip((C-R)/scale0, -1, 1)
            for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.3,0.8):
                z = p * np.exp(eta*rel)
                zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, Lfull)
                if ... update
        # rel1 = p*(C-R)
        rel1 = p*(C-R)
        scale1=float(np.max(np.abs(rel1)))
        if scale1 > 1e-300:
            rel=np.clip(p*(C-R)/scale1, -1,1)
            for eta in (0.1,0.3,1.0):
                z = p*np.exp(eta*rel)
                ...
        # Maybe previous additive direction -? Could also use h = C - R but without p for normals.
        # Accept
        if local_sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
            p=local_p; cur=local_sc; stall=0
            if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur; best_time=time.monotonic()
                if verbose: print(f"      iter {it} new best {best_sc:.6f}")
        else:
            stall += 1
            # maybe accept small worse after long to avoid? no
            if stall > 12 and rng.random() < 0.3:
                p=best.copy(); cur=best_sc
            if stall > 30 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                sigma=float(0.02 + 0.003*min(stall,40))
                z=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, Lfull)
                if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc*1.05:
                    p=zz; cur=sc
        # If stuck very long, try larger beta/power etc via it%7
        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 100 == 0:
            print(f"      iter {it} cur={cur:.6f} best={best_sc:.6f} stall={stall}")
        # early stop if near lower bound? lower=2n/(2n-1). If best_sc < lower+1e-6, break.
        lower=float(2*n/(2*n-1))
        if best_sc <= lower + 1e-7: break
    return best, best_sc
```

def _add_unique(seq, seen):
    for old in seen:
       if len(seq)==len(old) and float(np.abs(seq-old).max()) < 1e-7:
          return False
    seen.append(seq.copy())
    return True

def _is_duplicate(seq, seen):
    for old in seen:
        if len(seq)==len(old):
            if float(np.max(np.abs(seq-old))) < 1e-7: return True
    return False

Implement top-level `_freq_flatten(p, alpha, L)`:
```
def _freq_flatten(p, alpha, L):
    c=_full_conv(p,L); cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax<=0: return None
    r=c/cmax
    r=np.where(r>0,r,0)
    Cnew = cmax * np.power(r, 1.0/alpha)
    Cfreq=np.fft.rfft(Cnew,L)
    A=np.sqrt(Cfreq.astype(complex) - 0j?) # complex
    # enforce A[0] real positive: if angle near pi? Set phase?
    # We can adjust argument to reduce imaginary at 0? For irfft, a[0]? 
    A[0]=abs(A[0])
    a=np.fft.irfft(A,L)
    a=a[:len(p)]
    if (a<0).mean()>0.4: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None
    return a/s
```

def _listify(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).flatten()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    p=p/s
    return [float(v) for v in p]
```

`_random_initial` code:
```
def _random_initial(n, rng):
    kind=int(rng.integers(0,7))
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    if kind==0:
       A=float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.5))
       p=1.0 + A*np.cos(2*pi*t)
       if rng.random()<0.5:
          B=float(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6)); p += B*np.cos(4*pi*t)
       if rng.random()<0.5:
          C=float(rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4)); p += C*np.cos(6*pi*t)
    elif kind==1:
       x=2*t-1
       A=float(rng.uniform(0,8)); gamma=float(rng.uniform(0.3,3)); p=(1+A*x*x)**gamma
    elif kind==2:
       p=np.ones(n)*0.02
       K=int(rng.integers(1,6))
       for _ in range(K):
          c0=float(rng.uniform(0,1)); s=float(rng.uniform(0.005,0.2)); w=float(rng.lognormal(0,1))
          d=np.abs(t-c0); d=np.minimum(d,1-d)
          p += w*np.exp(-0.5*(d/s)**2)
       # maybe trend
    elif kind==3:
       K=int(rng.integers(2,12)); p=np.empty(n); heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K); edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); blocks=... fill array via loop.
    elif kind==4:
       p=np.ones(n); k=int(n*rng.uniform(0.1,0.8)); ratio=float(rng.uniform(1.5,8)); idx=rng.permutation(n); p[idx[:k]]=ratio; p *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
    elif kind==5:
       p=np.ones(n)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*pi,8)
       for m in range(1,8): amp=float(rng.normal(0, 1.0/m)); p += amp*np.cos(2*pi*m*t + ph[m-1])
    else:
       # random smooth lognormal
       p=rng.lognormal(0, rng.uniform(0.2,1.5), n)
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-8); p=p/p.sum(); return p
```
Need `np.pi` or import math? Use `np.pi`.
For kind 3 fill blocks: 
```
edges=np.concatenate(([0.0], rng.uniform(0,1,K-1), [1.0])) if K>1 else np.array([0,1])
for b in range(K):
  s=int(round(edges[b]*n)); e=max(s+1,int(round(edges[b+1]*n))); p[s:e]=heights[b]
```
No nested.

`rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)` requires K-1 positive. handle. For K=1? we choose 2..12.

Let's produce final code. Before final, let's re-evaluate mathematical correctness of J-step sign.
We want condition R_i=C. Let current residual b=C-R. If b positive, R too low. Jδ ≈ b. If we add δ positive to coordinate, R at that coordinate increases by Jδ_i (with first-order) so residual decreases. So δ=b/μ positive. Good. Multiplicative rel=C-R positive at low R; p*exp positive increase low R; negative high decrease. Good.
Now, does decreasing high R coordinates reduce max convolution? R_i is weighted sum of paired values for peaks. Yes.

Now, possible issue: `C=dot(w,c)` is weighted average of c, and `R_i` maybe on scale of values a not c. In uniform example, C=1/n, R=1/n. Good. For small support? R dimensions: c has dimensions a², R has a. C has a². Wait if sum a=1, a has dimensions 1/n, c has 1/n? Actually dimensionless. For one peak, c=∑ a_i a_j, each product ~1/n², sum n terms ->1/n. R_i=a_{k-i} ~1/n. So same scale. Good.

      if time.monotonic() > t_exp: break

      sc=_score_seq(p)
      if finite: cands.append
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    score(a) = 2 * n * max_k conv(a,a)_k / (sum(a))^2 .

The main local optimizer uses the optimality condition for the active peaks:
for active weights w, R_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j should be approximately equal to
C = sum_k w_k c_k.  It alternates between a regularized linearized projection
solving (J_w + mu I) delta = C - R, multiplicative mirror-descent steps based
on the logged gradient, occasional frequency-domain reciprocal-convolution
flattening, and random restarts/perturbations.  Multiple lengths and several
random parametric initializations are used to avoid uniform local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _sanitize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return p / s


def _init_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float) / int(n)


def _full_conv(a, L=None):
    n = len(a)
    nout = 2 * n - 1
    if nout <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if L is None:
        L = _next_pow2(nout)
    if n <= 128:
        return np.convolve(a, a).astype(float)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return c[:nout].astype(float)


def _score_seq(p, L=None):
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    c = _full_conv(p, L)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * len(p) * mx)


def _matvec_J(x, W, L):
    """
    Compute J_w x where J_w[i,j] = w[i+j].  This is the linear operator
    mapping changes in a to first-order changes in R_i = sum_j w[i+j] a_j.
    """
    n = len(x)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    y = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(X), L)
    return y[:n]


def _row_sums_J(w, n):
    """Row sums of J_w: row_i = sum_j w[i+j]."""
    pref = np.empty(len(w) + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(w, out=pref[1:])
    # i = 0..n-1, sum w[i : i+n]
    return pref[n:2 * n] - pref[:n]


def _make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m):
    m = len(c)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)

    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

    if method == 0:
        mtop = int(np.clip(top_m, 1, m))
        if mtop < m:
            kth = m - mtop
            idx = np.argpartition(c, kth)[-mtop:]
            w[idx] = c[idx]
        else:
            w = c.copy()
    elif method == 1:
        r = c / cmax
        r = np.where(r > 0.0, r, 0.0)
        if float(power) <= 1.0:
            w = r.copy()
        else:
            w = np.power(r, float(power))
        w += 1e-12
    elif method == 2:
        r = c / cmax
        e = np.exp(float(beta) * (r - 1.0))
        w = e + 1e-12
    else:
        eps = float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.25))
        thr = cmax * (1.0 - eps)
        w = np.where(c >= thr, c, 0.0)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=float)
        s = float(m)
    w /= s
    return w


def _cg_solve_J(W, row, R, C, mu, max_iter, tol, L):
    """
    Solve (J_w + mu I) delta = C - R using preconditioned CG.
    C and R are scalar/vector as in the optimizer.
    """
    n = len(R)
    b = C - R
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(b))
    if b_norm <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    r = b.copy()
    pref = float(mu) + row + 1e-12
    pref = np.where(pref > 1e-12, pref, 1.0)
    z = r / pref
    rho = float(np.dot(z, r))
    if not np.isfinite(rho) or rho <= 1e-300:
        return b / float(mu)

    pvec = z.copy()
    delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        Ap = _matvec_J(pvec, W, L) + float(mu) * pvec
        pAp = float(np.dot(pvec, Ap))
        if not np.isfinite(pAp) or pAp <= 1e-300:
            break
        alpha = rho / pAp
        if not np.isfinite(alpha):
            break
        delta += alpha * pvec
        r_new = r - alpha * Ap
        r_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(r_new))
        if not np.isfinite(r_norm):
            break
        r = r_new
        if r_norm <= tol * b_norm:
            break
        rho_new = float(np.dot(z, r))
        if not np.isfinite(rho_new) or rho_new <= 1e-300:
            break
        beta = rho_new / rho
        pvec = z + beta * pvec
        rho = rho_new

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
        delta = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, b / float(mu))
    return delta


def _eval_candidate(z, L):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if z.size == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
    s = float(z.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None, float("inf")
    z = z / s
    c = _full_conv(z, L)
    if c.size == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return None, float("inf")
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return z, float(2.0 * len(z) * mx)


def _freq_flatten_from_c(p, c, alpha, L):
    """
    Alternate move: target a flatter convolution C_new and take a complex
    square-root in the frequency domain, then project to non-negative support.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != 2 * len(p) - 1:
        return None
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return None
    r = c / cmax
    r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)
    r = np.maximum(r, 1e-12)
    if alpha < 1e-6:
        Cnew = cmax * np.ones_like(r)
    else:
        Cnew = cmax * np.power(r, 1.0 / float(alpha))
    Cnew = np.where(np.isfinite(Cnew), Cnew, 0.0)

    Cfreq = np.fft.rfft(Cnew, L)
    A = np.sqrt(Cfreq + 0j)
    A = np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0 + 0.0j)
    if A.size > 0:
        A[0] = float(np.abs(A[0]))
    if L % 2 == 0 and A.size == L // 2 + 1:
        A[-1] = float(np.abs(A[-1]))

    a = np.fft.irfft(A, L)[:len(p)]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    neg_frac = float(np.mean(a < 0.0))
    if neg_frac > 0.35:
        return None
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _random_initial(n, rng):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    kind = int(rng.integers(0, 7))

    if kind == 0:
        # Asymmetry-free trigonometric shapes, useful as a basin.
        A = float(rnguniform if False else rng.uniform(0.05, 1.6))
        B = 0.0
        Cc = 0.0
        p = 1.0 + A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            B = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
            p += B * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            Cc = float(rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.4))
            p += Cc * np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * t)
        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            p += float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)) * np.cos(8.0 * np.pi * t + float(rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)))
    elif kind == 1:
        # Polynomial-ish, often asymmetric after noise.
        x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
        A = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 8.0))
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
        p = (1.0 + A * x * x) ** gamma
    elif kind == 2:
        # Random smooth one/two-sided Gaussians, asymmetric.
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float) * 0.02
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        for _ in range(K):
            c0 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            s = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.22))
            w = float(rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0))
            d = np.abs(t - c0)
            d = np.minimum(d, 1.0 - d)
            p += w * np.exp(-0.5 * (d / s) ** 2)
    elif kind == 3:
        # Random blocks.
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 12))
        edges = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1), np.array([1.0])))
        edges.sort()
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        for b in range(K):
            s = int(round(edges[b] * n))
            e = max(s + 1, int(round(edges[b + 1] * n)))
            e = min(n, e)
            p[s:e] = heights[b]
    elif kind == 4:
        # Random two-level with arbitrary positions plus tiny noise.
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        k = int(n * float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.8)))
        ratio = float(rng.uniform(1.5, 8.0))
        idx = rng.permutation(n)
        p[idx[:k]] = ratio
        p *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
    elif kind == 5:
        # Smooth random Fourier series with random phases.
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        max_m = 8
        for m in range(1, max_m + 1):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / float(m)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)
    else:
        # Random lognormal baseline.
        p = rng.lognormal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.6)), n)

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-10)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _init_uniform(n)
    return p / s


def _resample(p, n_new):
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n_new = int(max(16, n_new))
    n = len(p)
    if n == n_new:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) / float(n_new)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return _init_uniform(n_new)
    return q / s


def _cand_sort_key(item):
    return item[1]


def _is_duplicate(seq, seen):
    for old in seen:
        if len(seq) == len(old):
            try:
                d = float(np.max(np.abs(seq - old)))
            except Exception:
                d = 1e9
            if d < 1e-7:
                return True
    return False


def _listify(p):
    p = _sanitize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = p / s
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def _optimize_sequence(p0, rng, budget_s, verbose=False):
    p = _sanitize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s < 10.0:
        sc = _score_seq(p)
        return p, sc

    n = len(p)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n + 2)
    c = _full_conv(p, L)
    cmax = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return p, float("inf")

    cur = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s

    it = 0
    stall = 0

    method = 1
    power = 8.0
    beta = 100.0
    top_m = max(4, int(n / 256))
    mu = 1.15

    if verbose:
        print(f"      optimizer start n={n} score={cur:.6f} budget={budget_s:.0f}s")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _full_conv(p, L)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if it % 7 == 0:
            u = float(rng.random())
            if u < 0.45:
                method = 1
                power = float(rng.choice([3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 12.0, 20.0, 35.0, 64.0, 128.0]))
            elif u < 0.72:
                method = 0
                top_m = int(np.clip(int(n * rng.choice([0.0003, 0.0007, 0.0015, 0.003, 0.006, 0.012, 0.03])), 1, n))
            elif u < 0.9:
                method = 2
                beta = float(rng.choice([20.0, 50.0, 120.0, 300.0, 800.0, 2000.0]))
            else:
                method = 3

            if stall > 20:
                mu = float(rng.choice([1.05, 1.08, 1.12, 1.20]))
            elif stall > 8:
                mu = float(rng.choice([1.10, 1.18, 1.30]))
            else:
                mu = float(rng.choice([1.15, 1.25, 1.45, 1.80]))

        w = _make_weights(c, n, rng, method, power, beta, top_m)
        row = _row_sums_J(w, n)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        R = _matvec_J(p, W, L)
        R = np.where(np.isfinite(R), R, 0.0)
        C = float(np.dot(w, c))
        if not np.isfinite(C) or C <= 1e-300:
            C0 = float(np.dot(p, R))
            C = C0 if np.isfinite(C0) and C0 > 1e-300 else float(cmax)

        local_p = p
        local_sc = cur_sc

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            # Linearized active-set projection.
            max_cg = 20 if n <= 4096 else 14
            delta = _cg_solve_J(W, row, R, C, mu, max_cg, 1e-8, L)
            if delta is not None:
                d = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, 0.0)
                dsum = float(d.sum())
                dctr = d - p * dsum
                med = float(np.median(p))
                maxd = 80.0 * max(med, 1e-12)
                d = np.clip(d, -maxd, maxd)
                dctr = np.clip(dctr, -maxd, maxd)

                for dv, alpha in ((dctr, 1.0), (d, 0.5), (dctr, 0.25), (d, 0.1)):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                        break
                    z = p + alpha * dv
                    zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, L)
                    if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                        local_p = zz
                        local_sc = sc

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            # Multiplicative step directly pushing R_i toward C.
            rel0 = C - R
            scale0 = float(np.max(np.abs(rel0)))
            if scale0 > 1e-300:
                rel = np.clip(rel0 / scale0, -1.0, 1.0)
                for eta in (0.10, 0.30, 0.80):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                        break
                    z = p * np.exp(float(eta) * rel)
                    zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, L)
                    if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                        local_p = zz
                        local_sc = sc

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            # Logger-style log gradient: proportional to a_i(C - R_i).
            rel1 = p * (C - R)
            scale1 = float(np.max(np.abs(rel1)))
            if scale1 > 1e-300:
                rel = np.clip(rel1 / scale1, -1.0, 1.0)
                for eta in (0.30, 1.00):
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                        break
                    z = p * np.exp(float(eta) * rel)
                    zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, L)
                    if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                        local_p = zz
                        local_sc = sc

        # Occasional spectrum-domain flattening.
        if stall >= 3 and rng.random() < 0.07 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
            for alphaf in (2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                pff = _freq_flatten_from_c(p, c, alphaf, L)
                if pff is not None:
                    zz, sc = _eval_candidate(pff, L)
                    if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                        local_p = zz
                        local_sc = sc

        # Rare random block scaling, useful to escape plateaus.
        if stall > 15 and rng.random() < 0.20 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
            bs = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 512), max(4, n // 16)))
            bs = min(bs, n)
            st = int(rng.integers(0, n - bs + 1))
            f = float(rng.choice([0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 1.6, 2.5, 6.0]))
            z = p.copy()
            z[st:st + bs] *= f
            zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, L)
            if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
                local_p = zz
                local_sc = sc

        if local_sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
            p = local_p
            cur = local_sc
            stall = 0
            if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best = p.copy()
                best_sc = cur
                if verbose:
                    print(f"        iter {it}: new best {best_sc:.6f} (n={n})")
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 12 and rng.random() < 0.30:
                p = best.copy()
                cur = best_sc
            if stall > 30 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                sigma = float(0.02 + 0.003 * min(stall, 40))
                z = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                zz, sc = _eval_candidate(z, L)
                if zz is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc * 1.05:
                    p = zz
                    cur = sc

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"        iter {it}: cur={cur:.6f} best={best_sc:.6f} stall={stall} n={n}")

        lower = float(2.0 * n / (2.0 * n - 1.0))
        if best_sc <= lower + 1e-7:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Top-level search.  Must return the best list[float] found before timeout.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 120.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    # Leave a safety margin for construction, official check, and weird overhead.
    deadline = start + max(120.0, budget) - 60.0

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(iseed % (2**31 - 1))

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"propose_candidate start, budget={budget:.0f}s")

    best_p = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    parent = None
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)  # noqa: F821
        parent = _sanitize(parent_raw)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        sc = _score_seq(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_p = parent.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            print(f"parent score: {best_sc:.6f} (n={len(parent)})")

    parent_n = len(parent) if parent is not None else 3072

    lengths = []
    candidate_lengths = [1024, 2048, 4096, 6144, 8192, parent_n]
    if parent_n > 0:
        candidate_lengths.append(max(1024, min(8192, 2 * parent_n)))
    for Lval in candidate_lengths:
        try:
            Lval = int(Lval)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if 512 <= Lval <= 8192 and Lval not in lengths:
            lengths.append(Lval)
    lengths.sort()

    cands = []
    if parent is not None:
        cands.append((parent.copy(), float(best_sc)))

    t_explore_end = min(deadline - 120.0, start + max(30.0, 0.045 * budget))

    inits_per_len = 12 if budget < 1200 else 18
    for n in lengths:
        for _ in range(inits_per_len):
            if time.monotonic() > t_explore_end:
                break
            p = _random_initial(n, rng)
            if p is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_seq(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                cands.append((p, sc))
        if time.monotonic() > t_explore_end:
            break

    if parent is not None:
        for n in lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > t_explore_end:
                break
            if n == len(parent):
                continue
            p = _resample(parent, n)
            if p is not None:
                sc = _score_seq(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cands.append((p, sc))

    if not cands:
        p = _init_uniform(2048)
        sc = _score_seq(p)
        cands.append((p, sc))

    cands.sort(key=_cand_sort_key)
    unique_inits = []
    for p, sc in cands:
        if _is_duplicate(p, unique_inits):
            continue
        unique_inits.append((p, sc))
        if len(unique_inits) >= 6:
            break

    print(f"exploration done. candidates={len(cands)} unique={len(unique_inits)} best={best_sc:.6f}")

    # Allocate most time to polishing the current global best, but first let
    # a few promising starts receive a fair amount of local optimization.
    reserve = max(150.0, 0.34 * budget)
    top_pool = max(0.0, deadline - start - reserve)
    fracs = [0.36, 0.22, 0.16, 0.10, 0.08, 0.08]

    for idx, item in enumerate(unique_inits):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - reserve - 10.0:
            break
        init_p, init_sc = item
        frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.05
        t_alloc = frac * top_pool
        t_alloc = min(t_alloc, deadline - time.monotonic() - reserve)
        if t_alloc < 15.0:
            break

        print("-" * 60)
        print(f"optimizing start {idx}: n={len(init_p)} init_score={init_sc:.6f} alloc={t_alloc:.0f}s")
        pout, sc = _optimize_sequence(init_p, rng, t_alloc, verbose=True)
        if pout is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_p = pout.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(f"  new global best: {best_sc:.6f} (n={len(best_p)})")

    # Final stage: optional resolution increase, then long polishing.
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_p is not None and time_left > 30.0:
        n_best = len(best_p)
        if n_best < 8192 and time_left > 320.0:
            n_up = min(8192, max(4096, 2 * n_best))
            p_up = _resample(best_p, n_up)
            if p_up is not None:
                sc_up = _score_seq(p_up)
                t_up = min(0.22 * time_left, 260.0)
                if t_up > 30.0:
                    print("-" * 60)
                    print(f"trying upsample: n={n_best} -> {n_up}, init_score={sc_up:.6f}, alloc={t_up:.0f}s")
                    pout, sc = _optimize_sequence(p_up, rng, t_up, verbose=True)
                    if pout is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_p = pout.copy()
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                        print(f"  upsample new global best: {best_sc:.6f} (n={n_best})")

        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left > 20.0:
            t_final = time_left - 20.0
            print("-" * 60)
            print(f"final polish: n={len(best_p)} current_best={best_sc:.6f} alloc={t_final:.0f}s")
            pout, sc = _optimize_sequence(best_p, rng, t_final, verbose=True)
            if pout is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_p = pout.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
                print(f"  final new global best: {best_sc:.6f} (n={len(best_p)})")

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = _init_uniform(2048)
        best_sc = _score_seq(best_p)

    out = _listify(best_p)
    if out is None:
        out = [1.0 / 2048.0] * 2048

    final_score = best_sc
    try:
        final_score = min(final_score, _score_seq(np.asarray(out, dtype=float)))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if 'evaluate_sequence' in globals():
        try:
            esc = float(evaluate_sequence(out))  # noqa: F821
            if np.isfinite(esc):
                final_score = min(final_score, esc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL: n={len(out)} estimated score={final_score:.6f}")
    print(f"runtime before return={time.monotonic() - start:.1f}s")
    return out
```
